			}
		}

			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 3
			owns_state = 615
			controls_state = 615
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 615
			controls_state = 615
			615 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_615_coal_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 58
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = coal
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			615 = { set_state_flag = has_615_templateResource_developed }
			615 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = coal
					amount = 14
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 374
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation2
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 374
			controls_state = 374
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 374
			controls_state = 374
			374 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_374_coal_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 45
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = coal
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			374 = { set_state_flag = has_374_templateResource_developed }
			374 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = coal
					amount = 12
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 531
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation3
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 531
			controls_state = 531
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 531
			controls_state = 531
			531 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_531_chromium_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 32
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = chromium
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			531 = { set_state_flag = has_531_templateResource_developed }
			531 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = chromium
					amount = 9
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 329
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation1
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 3
			owns_state = 329
			controls_state = 329
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 329
			controls_state = 329
			329 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_329_chromium_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 63
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = chromium
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			329 = { set_state_flag = has_329_templateResource_developed }
			329 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = chromium
					amount = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 620
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation4
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 620
			controls_state = 620
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 620
			controls_state = 620
			620 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_620_tungsten_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 17
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = tungsten
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			620 = { set_state_flag = has_620_templateResource_developed }
			620 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = tungsten
					amount = 4
				}
			}
		}
	}
